# Name-mismatch: the park's own website name (chat 9, Oct 29 9:25 PM; HQ 8:45 PM)

For the 868 parks tagged name-mismatch in data/us-key/why-not.json: the open Fable-list parks first (225; 223 have a POTA link). Source: the POTA park's official link (KPC-Data data/parkinfo/US-XX.json field 1) read by the look-up bot (text only). WEBSITE NAME = page title; HEADING = the first short line on the page that shares a word with POTA's name. Verdict: SAME NAME / PARTLY (some words) / DIFFERENT (try the website name as an alias under Rule G) / DEAD-BLOCKED. A page title is not an official boundary; R-G7 decides. Robots.txt obeyed. ℹ️ Maps and park info are a planning aid, not a guarantee.

Counts: {'PARTLY': 53, 'DIFFERENT': 39, 'SAME NAME': 82, 'DEAD/BLOCKED': 49}
